# OneInsure Service Support: full content export

Source: https://www.oneinsure.com/service (read on 30 September 2026). Covers the main page, all 56 service pages and every insurer page under them.

## 1. Main page: /service

> We understand that encountering service-related challenges can be daunting, especially during critical moments. That's why we've crafted this straightforward service support guide, designed to simplify the resolution process and provide you with clear steps to address any issues you may encounter.

Three categories, each listing its services (below). Contact line on the page: "Problem with your Policy? Get Assistance. Call 86559 86559".

## 2. Service pages (level 2), same on all 55 working pages

Every service page uses one identical template; only the title and breadcrumb change:

- **How do we help you?** Process Explanation ("We explain you the step by step procedure"), Procedural Guidance ("We guide you through the procedure and explore various options"), Manual Intervention ("We do it for you")
- **Help us understand your concern**, then **Select Insurance Company** (grid of insurer logos, each linking to the insurer page below)
- Callback form: Your Name, Mobile No., OTP, Email, "Please write your queries / concerns in detail…", buttons Submit / GET A CALL / CALL US

## 3. Insurer pages (level 3): the actual instructions

Each insurer page offers up to five ways to make the request (Website, Email, Call, SMS, Visit a branch), then "Important Things to Remember" and a "Need OneInsure Help?" form (Name, Mobile No, Email ID, City, Pincode, Date). Where several insurers share identical instructions for a service, they are grouped together below.

## Life Insurance

### Tax Certificate

URL: https://www.oneinsure.com/Service/Life-Tax-Certificate · 18 insurers

"Need OneInsure Help?" text: "A Tax Certificate serves as an investment proof to receive tax benefits. For guidance with your tax certificate, fill in your details and our customer support team will get in touch with you. You can also download the OneInsure app and download a Tax Certificate with just a few clicks."

#### LIC of India

**Website** (https://customer.onlinelic.in/LICEPS/Login/begin.do)

1. To get the Tax Certificate from Insurer's website, keep the following information handy
    - User ID
    - Password
2. Post submission of request, the Tax Certificate can be downloaded directly from the insurer's website
3. Please click on the below link to proceed

**Visit a branch**

1. Visit the nearest Branch and kindly carry the following documents:
    - Request Letter
    - Standard ID proof of Proposer
2. Post submission of request, the Tax Certificate will be handed over to you.
3. All documents should be self attested and submitted at

**Important things to remember**

- Tax certificates can be mailed to the registered email id
- As per current Tax regulations , maximum of Rs. 1,50,000/- can be claimed under section 80 C by an Individual.

#### Birla Sun Life Insurance

**Website** (https://lifeinsuranceservicing.adityabirlacapital.com/)

1. To obtain the Tax Certificate from the insurer's website, ensure you have the following information available for verification:

1. To obtain the Tax Certificate through email:

    - After the submission of the request, the Tax Certificate will be sent to you via email
2. Send the email to

    - Policy Number
    - Date of Birth
    - Other Details For Verification
2. Based on your request, the insurer will provide the soft copy of Tax Certificate through email. [ [Click Here] - If your email ID is not registered]
3. Please call at

1. To obtain the Tax Certificate, you can send the SMS from your registered mobile number. [ [Click Here] - If your mobile number is not registered]
    - SMS PPC <policynumber>
2. The Insurer will email the Tax Certificate to your registered Email Id. [#

**Important things to remember**

- If policy holder fails to pay the premium even after the expiry of grace period, the policy status will be changed to lapse or Paid- Up depending on the year in which the policyholder fails to pay the premium.

- Base policy benefits and rider benefits are not payable for a lapsed policy.
- All cheques to be marked as "A/c Payee".
- Cash payments are accepted upto Rs.49,999/- only.

### Freelook

URL: https://www.oneinsure.com/Service/Life-Freelook-Payout · 18 insurers

"Need OneInsure Help?" text: "Not happy with the terms and conditions of your insurance policy? Follow the steps mentioned above to know what to do or raise a service request through OneInsure and we will assist you through the free-look-period benefits."

#### LIC of India

**Visit a branch**

1. Visit the nearest Branch and kindly carry the following documents:
    - Freelook Form (available at branch)
    - Original Policy Bond (Indemnity bond in lieu of original policy bond)
    - Original Pre-printed cancelled cheque (incase NEFT details are not provided)
    - Standard ID proof of Proposer
2. Once the changes are done, the insurer will provide a confirmation
3. All documents should be self attested and submitted at

**Important things to remember**

- Request can be placed within Freelook period which is 15 days from the receipt of Policy document. For Distance Marketing policies, it is 30 days.
- For ULIPS the NAV is calculated for the same day when the freelook cancellation request is submitted.
- Any fluctuations in payout amount due to NAV has to be borne by the policyowner.

"Need OneInsure Help?" text: "Partial withdrawal is allowed if all due premiums have been paid on time and the policy is active. For any help regarding partial withdrawals, follow the steps mentioned above or contact us to raise a request through OneInsure."

#### LIC of India

1. Visit the nearest Branch and kindly carry the following documents:
    - Request Letter/Policy Servicing Form (available at branch)
    - Copy of CI or YPD (part of policy bond)
    - Original Pre-printed cancelled cheque
    - Standard ID proof of Proposer
2. Confirmation of the Partial Withdrawal will provided by the insurer
3. All documents should be self attested and submitted at

**Important things to remember**

- Before deciding on a partial withdrawal, one must verify the impact of such a withdrawal on the continuity of the policy.
- If the policy was taken under Married Women property Act, then the form must be signed by the Appointed trustee.

### Maturity/Survival

URL: https://www.oneinsure.com/Service/Life-Maturity-ClaimSurvival-Claim · 18 insurers

"Need OneInsure Help?" text: "Maturity benefit refers to the amount received by a policyholder or nominee on the maturity of a policy. To know how to receive this benefit, contact OneInsure by dropping in your details below."

#### LIC of India

**Visit a branch**

1. Visit the nearest Branch and kindly carry the following documents:
    - Request Letter/Policy Servicing Form (available at branch)
    - Original Pre-printed cancelled cheque
    - Original Policy Bond (If case of Maturity)
    - Standard ID proof of Proposer
2. Acknowledgment of the Maturity/Survival Benefit will offered by the insurer
3. All documents should be self attested and submitted at

**Important things to remember**

- Original policy document is required to claim the maturity amount. If missing/lost/not available, indemnity bond in lieu of the original policy document is required.
- TDS will be applicable as per provisions of section 194DA of Income Tax Act @2% in case valid PAN is provided, however, rate will be charged @ 20% in case valid PAN is not available with the Company.
- If there is any other request along with the maturity, like Address change, Bank details change or indemnity bond for lost policy then that request would be executed first, followed by a cooling off period of 15 days from the letter date and then fresh payout request to be done.

    - Standard ID proof of Proposer
2. Acknowledgment of the Maturity/Survival Benefit will offered by the insurer
3. All documents should be self attested and submitted at

**Important things to remember**

- Original policy document is required to claim the maturity amount. If missing/lost/not available, indemnity bond in lieu of the original policy document is required.
- TDS will be applicable as per provisions of section 194DA of Income Tax Act @2% in case valid PAN is provided, however, rate will be charged @ 20% in case valid PAN is not available with the Company.
- If there is any other request along with the maturity, like Address change, Bank details change or indemnity bond for lost policy then that request would be executed first, followed by a cooling off period of 15 days from the letter date and then fresh payout request to be done.

- Future premiums will not be accepted once the CCO is activated.

### Rider Addition

URL: https://www.oneinsure.com/Service/Life-Rider-Addition · 18 insurers

"Need OneInsure Help?" text: "A rider provides additional benefits over and above the base policy. To get details on how to add riders to your policy, fill the form given below and our customer support team will be happy to guide you with the same."

3. All documents should be self attested and submitted at

**Important things to remember**

- Rider cannot be added if life assured is minor.
- Riders are not allowed for NRI customers.
- Addition of rider is subject to Underwriting decision i.e depending on the nature of the rider and the sum assured additional medical tests can be called for.
- This feature is product specific and subject to underwriting.

#### LIC of India, Birla Sun Life Insurance, Max Life Insurance, AEGON Life Insurance, Bharti AXA Life Insurance, Tata AIA Life Insurance, Reliance Life Insurance, Edelweiss Tokio Life Insurance, Canara HSBC Life Insurance, IDBI Federal Life Insurance, DHFL Pramerica Life Insurance

    - Request Letter
    - Standard ID proof of Proposer
2. Once the changes are done, the insurer will provide a confirmation for the same
3. All documents should be self attested and submitted at

**Important things to remember**

- The reduction in premium will reduce the Sum Assured as per the regulatory limits.
- Reducing the Sum Assured will change the future benefits in the policy.
- For assigned policies, assignee's signature is required.
- Request needs to be submitted 15 days prior to the due date.
- Reduction in premium is not allowed in Paid Up status.
- Miminum premium cannot be less than the minimum premium allowed in the plan.
- Policy servicing charges may be levied as applicable. Please refer to your policy document for details.
- This feature is product specific and subject to underwriting.

### Addition Of Member

URL: https://www.oneinsure.com/Service/Health-Addition-of-Member · 22 insurers

"Need OneInsure Help?" text: "You can add members to your health policy by following the procedure mentioned above. You can also inquire with the OneInsure team by filling the provided form. Optionally, you can install the OneInsure app, where you may raise a request to add a member with a few taps."

#### Star Allied, HDFC ERGO, Bajaj Allianz, ICICI Lombard, ManipalCigna, Royal Sundaram, Bharti AXA, Tata AIG, Iffco Tokio, Reliance

**Visit a branch**

1. Visit the nearest Branch and kindly carry the following documents:
    - Request Letter
    - Proposal Form
    - Marriage Certificate(In case of marriage)
    - Adoption Papers(In case of Adopted Child)
    - Birth Certificate(In case of new born)
    - Premium Cheque
2. Once the member is added, the insurer will share a confirmation through courier or email. [ [Click Here] - If Email ID is not registered]
3. Please submit all documents at

**Important things to remember**

- Member who can be add to health policy are spouse,children and parents.
- Such additions can happen generally at policy renewal although deletion can happen at any point of time.
- Newely added member will be subjected to fresh waiting periods.

**Important things to remember**

### Porting of Policy

URL: https://www.oneinsure.com/Service/Health-PORTING-OF-POLICY · 22 insurers

"Need OneInsure Help?" text: "You can port your health plan after the completion of one policy year. Let us help you port to a better plan. Fill the form to get a call back from a OneInsure expert. Optionally, you can install the OneInsure app, where you may raise a request to port your policy with a few taps."

#### Star Allied

**Website** (https://www.starhealth.in/portability)

1. To get Porting of Policy through the Insurer's website, keep the following information handy
    - Name
    - Previous policy expiry date
    - Email Id
    - Phone number
    - Product type
    - All Insured with same insurer(Y/N)
2. Please click the below link to proceed

**Visit a branch**

1. Visit the nearest Branch and kindly carry the following documents:
    - New Proposal form
    - Duly filled Portability form
    - Latest Renewal Notice
    - Previous 4 years Policy copies
    - All Insured members KYC
    - Premium Cheque
2. Insurer will send the policy document through courier
3. Compare your current plan with other products

**Important things to remember**

- All individual health policy holder can now change/port their current policy to a new policy with a new insurer under the portability guidelines.
- If the new insurer accepts your request to port,then the continunty benefits for organ specific surgeries/treatment and PED's will continue and no fresh waiting period will be levied.
- The application should be made 45 days prior to the current renewal date of your policy.
- Its is advisable to take a short term policy from the current insurer in the event of your portability request remain pending with insurer with your renewal date approaching.

    - Fresh Proposal
    - Consent letter of Old Owner for transfer of policy
    - Endorsed RC Copy or Sales Deed along with 29/30 form/RTO Transfer fee paid receipt.
    - Death Certificate of current registered & Legal heir certificate (in case of Death of Vehicle owner)
    - Premium Cheque (If required)

3. All documents should be self attested and submitted at

    - Death Certificate of current registered & Legal heir certificate (in case of Death of Vehicle owner)

3. Please click on below link to proceed

**Email** (care@hdfcergo.com)
